# Feature parity

This table lists every feature of the reference product that we found in its
public documentation (and, later, in a logged-in test account). See
`docs/clean-room.md` for how evidence is collected and cited.

Status values:

- **done (CalDAV)**: implemented, and a passing full UI E2E test against a
  real CalDAV calendar proves it (real events created, moved and deleted;
  reload and persistence checked). The last column names the tests.
- **done (Google)**: done (CalDAV), and every test that proves the row also
  passed against a Google Calendar account in the recorded Google run
  (`docs/e2e-google-run.md`, test list in `docs/e2e-google-run.tsv`). A row
  with a test that skipped on Google, or a test added after the run, stays
  done (CalDAV). The two counts do not overlap: done (CalDAV) counts the rows
  proven on CalDAV only.
- **partial**: implemented, but only unit tests prove it, or a part of the
  spec is missing. The last column says which.
- **blocked**: not implemented yet, or a non-goal. The last column gives the
  reason.

Evidence ids (`E-…`) refer to the private evidence index. Spec links point to
`docs/specs/`.

## Summary

- Rows: 963
- done (CalDAV): 349
- done (Google): 233
- partial: 17
- blocked: 364

"done (CalDAV)" means a full UI E2E test passes against a real CalDAV server (Radicale in a
loopback container): Playwright drives the Tempoloom UI, the real API and scheduler write to the
calendar, and the test checks the calendar, a page reload and persistence. The suite runs in CI
on every push. "done (Google)" rows passed the same tests against Google Calendar too, in the
run recorded in docs/e2e-google-run.md; rows whose tests skip on Google stay "done (CalDAV)".

Evidence basis: every row comes from public evidence only (marketing pages and help docs). No
logged-in observation of the reference product was made, so behavior that only a logged-in
account shows (exact defaults, API shapes, current-version details) is "public-evidence only";
the open questions in each spec list what is unknown.

Tempoloom sends the update email itself, with an iCalendar REQUEST attached, because it writes changes without the provider's update emails; the limit uses the earlier of the old and new start. Not proven on Google Calendar: 1 of these tests did not pass in the Google run (skipped there, or added after it; docs/e2e-google-run.md). |

Tempoloom builds both planned notices: the at-risk digest (row 376) and an email that offers each new recurring series once, with a link to the meeting form filled from it. | 319 | Flexible meeting: attach rules to an existing recurring meeting | E-0176 | [meetings-flexible.md#setup](specs/meetings-flexible.md#setup) | done (CalDAV) | Full UI E2E passes on CalDAV (Radicale): “rules attach to an existing recurring meeting: the form fills from it, the series keeps its id, and a conflict moves only its own instance”. On CalDAV a moved instance is an override (RECURRENCE-ID) inside the one series; turning the meeting off or deleting it keeps the series as a normal recurring event. Custom is every N days, weeks or months (N 1 to 52), counted from the start date or the creation day; monthly is a day of the month or an Nth weekday; a month without its ideal day, or whose ideal day has passed, gets no new occurrence (unit test); a month whose ideal day is past the planning window waits for it; an adopted series repeats daily or every N weeks from its first instance. Not proven on Google Calendar: 2 of these tests did not pass in the Google run (skipped there, or added after it; docs/e2e-google-run.md). An edit of the rules, a rank or priority change, and an hours change of the organizer or of an attendee who is a user here place future occurrences again, outside the move limit; an edit of the title, notes or location keeps the times, and an attendee's time zone change or new meeting hours set also places again (unit tests). The count is per meeting in a rolling 24 hours and counts moves for conflicts and declines; a flag is raised only when another time exists. Not proven on Google Calendar: 2 of these tests did not pass in the Google run (skipped there, or added after it; docs/e2e-google-run.md). Reference note: current version. |

Rules attached to an existing series leave the series on the calendar, so that delete offers no cancel email. | 764 | Library entry 46: Lunch (habit) | E-0001 | [automations-library.md#concepts-and-inputs](specs/automations-library.md#concepts-and-inputs) | done (Google) | Full UI E2E passes on CalDAV (Radicale) and on Google Calendar (docs/e2e-google-run.md): “library habit entries 11, 35, 36, 43, 45, 46 add habits with their presets: wrap-up at the end of work hours, Lunch inside 11:30 to 14:00”; “a habit entry fills the habit form; saving places it and shows Added; a second use gets a suffix; delete clears the badge”. The 11:30 to 14:00 window is item-specific hours on workdays. Reference note: title-only evidence; preset proposed. |
| 765 | Delegated access for assistants | E-0074, E-0099, E-0003 | [teams-executive-assistant-use.md#behavior](specs/teams-executive-assistant-use.md#behavior) | done (CalDAV) | Full UI E2E passes on CalDAV (Radicale): “the delegate switches into the owner's account, sees the banner on every page, plans a task on the owner's calendar, and exits”; “the assistant sets up a flexible meeting for the executive, on the executive's calendar only”; “an assistant with two executives switches between them; a habit lands on the chosen executive's calendar only”. Executive assistant journey T1, T2 and T4; T3 (booking pages directory) is row 766. T2 sets up a new flexible meeting: converting an existing calendar meeting is not built. Not proven on Google Calendar: 3 of these tests did not pass in the Google run (skipped there, or added after it; docs/e2e-google-run.md). Reference note: owned by accounts-delegated-access.md. |
| 766 | Team booking pages directory | E-0074 | [teams-executive-assistant-use.md#behavior](specs/teams-executive-assistant-use.md#behavior) | done (CalDAV) | Full UI E2E passes on CalDAV (Radicale): “any member finds every team member's booking page and public links in the directory and copies one”; “the delegate switches into the owner's account, sees the banner on every page, plans a task on the owner's calendar, and exits”. Not proven on Google Calendar: 2 of these tests did not pass in the Google run (skipped there, or added after it; docs/e2e-google-run.md). Reference note: legacy only. |
| 767 | Three default booking links for a new user | E-0074 | [teams-executive-assistant-use.md#behavior](specs/teams-executive-assistant-use.md#behavior) | done (Google) | Full UI E2E passes on CalDAV (Radicale) and on Google Calendar (docs/e2e-google-run.md): “a new booking page gets three starter links with their priorities and durations”. The starter set follows booking-links.md rule 2, which owns this rule. Reference note: legacy only; owned by booking-links.md. |
| 768 | Habit templates common for executives | E-0074 | [teams-executive-assistant-use.md#behavior](specs/teams-executive-assistant-use.md#behavior) | done (CalDAV) | Full UI E2E passes on CalDAV (Radicale): “a monthly habit is placed once, on a day of the month or on the nth weekday”; “a habit entry fills the habit form; saving places it and shows Added; a second use gets a suffix; delete clears the badge”; “team habit templates show at the top of every member's habits page; edits and deletes never change habits made from them; only admins manage them”. The monthly reviews are habits with the monthly frequency; the other executive routines are library entries and team habit templates. Not proven on Google Calendar: 2 of these tests did not pass in the Google run (skipped there, or added after it; docs/e2e-google-run.md). Reference note: legacy only; owned by habits-automation.md. |
| 769 | Current version baseline (planner, chat, preview, automations, issues, insights, MCP) | E-0183, E-0148, E-0043 | [platform-current-version.md#behavior](specs/platform-current-version.md#behavior) | blocked | Needs the assistant chat, which is not built; the other parts of the baseline are built (preview mode, automations, issues, insights, MCP server). |
| 770 | Upgrade banner and self-serve upgrade from legacy | E-0183 | [platform-current-version.md#behavior](specs/platform-current-version.md#behavior) | blocked | Not implemented yet. Reference note: not needed: no legacy users. |
| 771 | Tasks suggested in focus time instead of own events | E-0183 | [platform-current-version.md#behavior](specs/platform-current-version.md#behavior) | done (Google) | Full UI E2E passes on CalDAV (Radicale) and on Google Calendar (docs/e2e-google-run.md): “with suggestions only, a focus time block lists the tasks to work on and tasks get no events”. Reference note: owned by planning-tasks.md. |
| 772 | Recommendations and issues list | E-0183, E-0148 | [platform-current-version.md#behavior](specs/platform-current-version.md#behavior) | done (CalDAV) | Full UI E2E passes on CalDAV (Radicale): “each built-in check raises its issue in the planner Issues tab, never edits an event, and resolves when its cause ends”; “an agenda draft is staged and written only when applied; recommendations list a skipped habit, an unmet focus goal and a day with too many meetings”. Issues come from the meeting checks; recommendations come from the planner's report and days with 6 hours or more of meetings. Not proven on Google Calendar: 2 of these tests did not pass in the Google run (skipped there, or added after it; docs/e2e-google-run.md). Reference note: detection in automations-meeting-quality.md. |
| 773 | Import from a closed competitor's export file | E-0182, E-0075 | [platform-current-version.md#behavior](specs/platform-current-version.md#behavior) | blocked | Non-goal by decision (billing, payments or mobile apps). Reference note: non-goal: source closed, format unknown. |
| 774 | Advice to turn off other scheduling tools first | E-0182, E-0075 | [platform-current-version.md#behavior](specs/platform-current-version.md#behavior) | done (Google) | Full UI E2E passes on CalDAV (Radicale) and on Google Calendar (docs/e2e-google-run.md): “a new user sees the advice to turn off other scheduling tools until they dismiss it”. A planner hint with a help page, until the user dismisses it. Reference note: proposed onboarding hint. |
